directory. Everything below describes the state of `apps/web` **on disk right now**. Nothing was
deleted. I did not deploy, did not touch `PLAN.md`, and did not run `pnpm build` after the UI
landed (see *Half-done* below).

| Check | Result |

| `npx vitest run` | **4 files / 41 tests passed** (`api.test.ts`, `config.test.ts`, `reducer.test.ts`, `demo.test.ts`) |

| `modal deploy` | **not run** — blocked by the handoff |

> The local test wrapper (`rtk`) swallows vitest's stdout and writes
> `apps/web/.vitest/json/output.json` asynchronously, so that JSON is often one run stale. Trust the
> console summary line, not the JSON file, unless you re-read it after the process exits.

## 2. What exists under apps/web

### Toolchain / scaffold
- `pnpm create vite@latest . --template react-ts` → **React 19.3, Vite 8.3, TypeScript 6.0**.
- Tailwind **v4** via `@tailwindcss/vite` (no `tailwind.config.js`; theme lives in `src/index.css`).
- shadcn/ui initialised with `pnpm dlx shadcn@latest init -d`, style **`base-nova`**, then
  `add card badge tabs scroll-area separator select table tooltip skeleton` (+ `button` from init).
  **This shadcn generation is Base UI–based, not Radix** — `@base-ui/react@1.8.0`, and `cn` comes
  from the standalone `cn` package, not `@/lib/utils`. API differences that bit me:
  - `<Select>` needs an `items={[{value,label}]}` prop on the Root for `<SelectValue/>` to render a
    label; `onValueChange` receives `unknown`.
  - `<Tabs>` uses `Tabs.Tab` / `Tabs.Panel` under the hood; `TabsList` takes `variant="line"`.
  - `<Badge>`/`<TooltipTrigger>` use Base UI's `render={<El/>}` slot pattern, not `asChild`.
- `tsconfig.app.json` deviations, both forced: `baseUrl` removed (TS 6 errors `TS5101`; `paths` alone
  resolves relative to the tsconfig) and `erasableSyntaxOnly: false` (the generated client code uses
  constructor parameter properties).
- `src/components/ui/scroll-area.tsx`: the unused `import * as React` was deleted (`noUnusedLocals`).
- `package.json` scripts: `dev`, `build`, `preview`, `lint` (oxlint), `test` (`vitest run`),
  `test:watch`. `packageManager: pnpm@9.15.4`.

### Library (`src/lib`) — the part worth reviewing
| File | What it does |
|---|---|
| `types.ts` | Mirror of `faultline_common/schemas.py`. **No field renamed.** I appended the MCP tool payload types (`RunCommandOutput`, `ReadFileOutput`, `WriteFileOutput`, `DirEntry`, `ListDirOutput`, `FaultPlan`) — these describe what the JSON string in `tool.result.data.output` parses into. |
| `log.ts` | Unified JSON-lines logger, `svc:"web"`, identical field order to `log.py`. Console + a 2000-line ring buffer with `subscribeLogs()`. |
| `config.ts` | `resolveConfig()` → `{harnessUrl, source}`, precedence window → `/config.json` → `VITE_HARNESS_URL` → built-in default. Rejects non-`http(s)` values; strips trailing slashes. Fully injectable for tests. |
| `api.ts` | `HarnessClient` (health / scenarios / createRun / getRun, `credentials:'omit'`, `HarnessError` carries `status`) and `subscribeRun()` — see §4. |
| `reducer.ts` | **The core.** Pure `reduce(state, Event) → ViewState`, plus `reduceAll`, `fromRunRecord`, selectors (`allCalls`, `faultCount`, `recoveredCount`, `elapsedMs`, `isTerminal`). |
| `format.ts` | duration/bytes/tokens/time formatting, fault blurbs, `shortSha`. |
| `replay.ts` | `DEMOS` registry, `loadDemo()`, `playReplay()` at `DEMO_STEP_MS = 400`. |

**Reducer derivations that must not be casually changed** — they are deliberately aligned with
`services/sandbox-env/GRADING.md` so the badge in the UI and the grader's checks agree:
- `normalisePath` strips quotes, `./`, `/workspace/`, and a trailing `/`.
- `commandIsMutating` uses GRADING.md's regex verbatim (`>`/`>>`/`tee`/`sed -i`/`rm`/`mv`/`cp`/
  `touch`/`mkdir`/`truncate`/`patch`/`git apply|checkout|reset|restore`) plus a `python -c … open(…,'w'|'a')` case.
- `commandIsRead` = not mutating **and** matches a read verb (`cat|head|tail|grep|rg|wc|diff|less|cmp|sha256sum|md5sum|ls|stat|find|pytest|nl|od`, `sed -n`, `python -m pytest`).
- `callTouches` does argv-token matching for `run_command`, direct path match otherwise.
- **`recovered`** (the "recovered" badge): a faulted call is recovered iff a later call on the same
  path succeeded **and** a successful *read* of that path happened at or before that success.
  Retrying blind does not count — that is exactly what `lost-ack` grades.
- `tool.result` without a matching `tool.call` synthesises a call, so a reconnect that loses the
  call frame still renders the output.
- Duplicate `tool_use_id` and `id <= lastEventId` are ignored (SSE replay after resume).

### UI
- `src/App.tsx` — header + query-param routing, **no router dependency**: `?run=<id>` → live view,
  `?demo=<id>` → replay, otherwise Home. `pushState` + `popstate`. Wrapped in `TooltipProvider`.
- `src/pages/Home.tsx` — health banner, model `<Select>` (`claude-haiku-4-5` default), scenario grid,
  bundled-replay buttons. `POST /runs` then navigates to `?run=<run_id>`.

- `src/components/`: `HealthBanner`, `ScenarioGrid` (+`ScenarioSkeletons`), `FaultBadge`
  (`FaultKindBadge` / `FaultFiredBadge` / `RecoveredBadge`), `Timeline`, `WorkspacePanel`,
  `LogsPanel`, `ScoreCard`, `RunHeader`.
- `src/hooks/useHarness.ts` — resolves config once, then `/health` + `/scenarios` via
  `Promise.allSettled`; both are allowed to fail (see §6). **Note:** it exposes `health`, not a
  `reachable` boolean — Home uses `harness.health?.ok === true`.
- `src/hooks/useRunView.ts` — `useRunView(client, source)` → `{state, transport, loadError, loading, replaying}`.
  `RunSource = {kind:'none'} | {kind:'live',runId} | {kind:'demo',demoId}`.
  **A file named `useRunStream.ts` with a `{kind:'demo',scenarioId}` variant existed briefly and is
  gone; `RunView.tsx`/`App.tsx` import `useRunView`.** If you reinstate `useRunStream`, keep the
  fix I applied to it: the live path must subscribe to SSE **even when the seeding `GET /runs/{id}`
  throws** — a run created a second ago can 404 briefly, and the original code skipped the
  subscription entirely in that case.
- Auto-scroll: `Timeline` sticks to the bottom while live and releases as soon as the reader
  scrolls up more than 80px.

### Demo mode
- `public/demo/lost-ack.json` — a **hand-written but contract-faithful** `RunRecord`, 32 events:
  read `CHANGELOG.md` → write it → **ack lost** (`504`, `is_error`, `fault.ack_lost`, 3042 ms) →
  read back → section already present so **no second write** → bump `version.py` → `pytest` 6 passed
  → `submit` → `episode.evaluated` 100/100 → `run.finished`. Two `workspace.diff` events with real
  unified diffs; seven mirrored `log` lines from `harness`/`sandbox-env`. File sizes and sha256s are
  computed from the real fixture at `services/sandbox-env/fixtures/ratelimiter`.

  (one `ack_lost`, `recovered=true`, exactly one CHANGELOG write, one `## [0.2.0]` heading,
  score 100, two diffs, non-empty logs). This is what stops the replay rotting.
- `scripts/export_demo.py` (repo root, mine) — reads `runs/<ts>_<run_id>/run.json`, validates

## 4. The harness contract the UI consumes

Base URL resolved by `src/lib/config.ts` (see §6). All requests are plain CORS, `credentials:'omit'`.

| Route | Request | Response the UI expects |
|---|---|---|
| `GET /health` | — | `Health` — `{svc, ok, version, has_provider_key, model_default?, sandbox_env_url?, detail}`. The banner renders `model_default` and shows a green "no provider key on api" badge when `has_provider_key === false`. |
| `GET /scenarios` | — | `Scenario[]` — `{id, title, description, task_prompt, max_steps, fault_kinds[]}`. `fault_kinds` drives the badges; an empty array just renders no badges. |
| `POST /runs` | `{scenario_id, model?, seed?, max_steps?}` | **`{run_id}`** at minimum. The UI navigates to `?run=<run_id>` immediately and does not read any other field. |
| `GET /runs/{id}` | — | full `RunRecord`. Used to seed the view (page refresh mid-run) **and** as the polling fallback body. |
| `GET /runs/{id}/events` | SSE | see below |

`RunRecord` fields the UI reads: `run_id, status, scenario_id, model, seed, max_steps, episode_id,
created_at, finished_at, events[], evaluation, usage{input_tokens,output_tokens}, error`.
Record-level fields **win** over what the events implied (`fromRunRecord`).

`Event` = `{id:number, ts:string, run_id:string, type:EventType, step?:number|null, data:object}`.
`data` shapes consumed (all fields tolerated as missing):
- `run.started` → `{scenario_id, model, seed, max_steps, anthropic_workspace?}`
- `episode.reset` → `{episode_id, files:FileEntry[], task_prompt}`
- `turn.text` → `{text}`
- `tool.call` → `{tool, input, tool_use_id}`
- `tool.result` → `{tool_use_id, tool?, output:string, is_error:boolean, duration_ms, fault?:FaultFired}`
  — `output` is a **JSON string** for structured tools (`stdout/stderr/exit_code/duration_ms/truncated`,
  or `content/size/sha256`, or `bytes_written/sha256`, or `entries[]`, or `{error, code, path}`);
  plain text is accepted and rendered as-is.
- `fault.fired` → a `FaultFired` **at the top level of `data`** (`{step, kind, path, mode}`);
  `data.fault` is also accepted.
- `workspace.diff` → `{files:FileEntry[], diffs:[{path, unified}]}` — **replaces** the snapshot.
- `episode.evaluated` → the whole `EvaluateResponse` (`{episode_id, score, passed, checks[], tests{}, ledger[]}`).
- `run.finished` → `{status, usage, duration_ms, error?}`
- `log` → a unified log line (`{ts, svc, lvl, ev, msg, …}`), rendered in the Logs tab.

### SSE resume semantics implemented in `subscribeRun()`
- Opens `GET {base}/runs/{id}/events`; when resuming it appends `?last_event_id=<n>` **as well as**
  relying on the browser's own `Last-Event-ID` header (EventSource cannot set headers, and we
  re-open the stream ourselves rather than letting it auto-retry, so the retry policy is testable).
  **The harness should honour either.**
- Listens on `message` *and* on every `EventType` as a named event, so the server may name frames or not.
- `event: done` ⇒ the run is over: close, no reconnect.
- Any other close is treated as the **~110 s rotation**, *if the stream delivered ≥1 event*: reconnect
  immediately with the resume point, error counter stays 0.
- A close that delivered **nothing** increments an error counter; after **2** consecutive such
  failures it demotes to polling `GET /runs/{id}` every **1500 ms**, emitting only events with
  `id > lastEventId`, and stops when `status ∈ {ok, error, truncated}`.
- Events are de-duplicated by `id`, so a replay from an earlier resume point is harmless.
- **Requirement on the harness:** `Event.id` must be the 0-based, contiguous, monotonic sequence
  within the run, and it must be the SSE frame id. `export_demo.py` enforces the same invariant.

### `/config.json`
Served from the site root, read once at startup, `cache: 'no-store'`:
```json
{ "harnessUrl": "https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-harness-api.modal.run" }
```
Precedence: `window.__FAULTLINE_CONFIG__.harnessUrl` → `/config.json` → `VITE_HARNESS_URL`
(build-time) → the hardcoded default. Non-`http(s)` values at any level are skipped, not used.
The resolved source is shown in the health banner (`config: window|config.json|build-env|default`).

---

## 5. Modal static hosting — what I learned

- Target URL shape (workspace `appliedlabsai`, env `local`, Server named `site`):
  `https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-web-site.modal.run`.
- `@app.server(image=…, unauthenticated=True, port=8000, min_containers=1, startup_timeout=60, name="site")`
  exists in modal 1.5.5; `modal.Server.from_name(app, name).get_url()` reads the URL back.
  Fallback if it misbehaves: `@app.function(...)` + `@modal.web_server(8000, startup_timeout=60, label="site")`.
- **`add_local_dir(..., copy=False)` (the default) mounts at container start, i.e. *after* the image
  is built** — so a `run_commands("cd /src && pnpm build")` would run against an empty `/src`.
  Any in-image build must use `copy=True`. The `modal_app.py` on disk already documents this and
  offers a prebuilt path (copy `dist/` in, no node in the image) plus an in-image build path.
- Keep the harness URL **out of the bundle**: write `/site/config.json` from `$HARNESS_URL` in
  `@modal.enter()`. Redeploying with a different `HARNESS_URL` then needs no frontend rebuild.
- Node/pnpm version traps on this machine, worth pinning in the image:
  - Local `node` is **v21.6.0** and local `pnpm` is **8.6.10**. `create-vite@9` needs
    `node:util.styleText` ⇒ **node ≥ 22**; I used `~/.nvm/versions/node/v24.13.1/bin/node`.
  - The committed `pnpm-lock.yaml` is **lockfileVersion 9.0** (written by pnpm 9.15.4 from the
    corepack cache). `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile` in the image must therefore run **pnpm 9 or
    10** — pin `npm install -g pnpm@9.15.4`, not a bare `pnpm@9`, and never pnpm 8.
  - Vite 8 requires node `^20.19 || >=22.12`; the nodesource `setup_22.x` line satisfies that.
- Modal web endpoints hard-cap one request at **150 s** (then a 303 that breaks CORS) — this is why
  the SSE client above is built around a ~110 s rotation plus resume, and why a polling fallback exists.

---

## 6. Design intent worth preserving

- **A dead harness must still leave a usable page.** `/health` and `/scenarios` failures are state,
  not exceptions; the banner says so in plain words and points at the bundled replay, which needs no
  backend and no provider key. Please do not turn these into a blocking error screen.
- **The reducer is the single source of truth** for both live and replay, which is what makes the
  demo trustworthy as a stand-in.
- **`recovered` is a claim about behaviour, not about success** — it deliberately refuses to light up
  when the agent retried without reading. Weakening it would make the UI disagree with the grader.
- Dark theme is default (`<html class="dark">`), no theme toggle. Typography is Geist (bundled
  locally via `@fontsource-variable/geist`, no CDN — matters because Modal serves this statically).

## 7. If you want my work as a starting point but a different structure

The only pieces I would fight to keep are `src/lib/reducer.ts` + its tests, `src/lib/api.ts`'s
`subscribeRun` reconnect/fallback policy + its tests, `public/demo/lost-ack.json` + `demo.test.ts`,
and `scripts/export_demo.py`. Everything else is presentation and can be rewritten freely.

Tool reply2026-09-12T21:58:23.532Z
"""Shared wire contracts for Faultline (pydantic v2).

Three consumers: services/sandbox-env (gym + MCP), services/agent-harness (model loop + API),
scripts/ (smoke + evidence). apps/web mirrors these in src/lib/types.ts — keep both in sync.

Sections: faults & scenarios · MCP tool I/O · ledger · gym REST · harness runs/events · persistence · health.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Literal

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

FaultKind = Literal["missing_file", "denied_write", "ack_lost"]
FaultMode = Literal["sticky", "transient"]

class FaultSpec(BaseModel):
    """One injected failure. Lives ONLY in sandbox-env state (never inside the command sandbox).

    sticky    : applied at reset (e.g. the file is really deleted) and never lifted.
    transient : intercepted at the tool boundary for the first `hits` matching calls, then lifted.
    """

    kind: FaultKind
    path: str = Field(description="workspace-relative path the fault targets")
    mode: FaultMode = "transient"
    hits: int | None = Field(default=1, description="matching calls affected before the fault lifts (None = forever)")
    delay_ms: int = Field(default=3000, description="ack_lost only: how long to hold the response before failing it")

class FaultPlan(BaseModel):
    faults: list[FaultSpec] = Field(default_factory=list)

class Scenario(BaseModel):
    """Public scenario fields (safe to show the agent and the browser)."""

    id: str
    title: str
    description: str = Field(description="one-paragraph human description shown in the UI")
    task_prompt: str = Field(description="what the agent is asked to do (goes into the user turn)")
    max_steps: int = 20
    fault_kinds: list[FaultKind] = Field(default_factory=list, description="which kinds are in play (not where/when)")

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- MCP tool I/O

ToolName = Literal["run_command", "read_file", "write_file", "list_dir"]
ErrorCode = Literal["ENOENT", "EACCES", "ETIMEDOUT", "EINVAL", "EINTERNAL", "ENOEPISODE"]

STDIO_CAP = 8_000  # chars per stream in run_command
CONTENT_CAP = 32_000  # chars in read_file

class RunCommandInput(BaseModel):
    command: str = Field(description="bash -lc <command>, cwd=/workspace")
    timeout_s: int = Field(default=30, ge=1, le=60)

class RunCommandOutput(BaseModel):
    stdout: str
    stderr: str
    exit_code: int
    duration_ms: int
    truncated: bool = False

class ReadFileInput(BaseModel):
    path: str

class ReadFileOutput(BaseModel):
    path: str
    content: str
    size: int
    sha256: str
    truncated: bool = False

class WriteFileInput(BaseModel):
    path: str
    content: str
    mode: Literal["overwrite", "append"] = "overwrite"

class WriteFileOutput(BaseModel):
    path: str
    bytes_written: int
    sha256: str = Field(description="sha256 of the whole file after the write")

class ListDirInput(BaseModel):
    path: str = "."

class DirEntry(BaseModel):
    name: str
    type: Literal["file", "dir", "other"]
    size: int | None = None

class ListDirOutput(BaseModel):
    path: str
    entries: list[DirEntry]

class ToolError(BaseModel):
    """Returned (as the tool result text, is_error=True) for both real and injected failures.

    The agent cannot tell injected from real: same shape, same wording as the OS/HTTP layer would use.
    """

    error: str = Field(description="human-readable, OS/HTTP-style, e.g. 'cat: config/settings.json: No such file or directory'")
    code: ErrorCode
    path: str | None = None
    detail: str | None = None

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- ledger (private to sandbox-env; public subset via observe)

class FaultFired(BaseModel):
    step: int
    kind: FaultKind
    path: str
    mode: FaultMode

class LedgerEntry(BaseModel):
    step: int
    ts: str
    tool: ToolName
    args_digest: str = Field(description="sha256[:12] of canonical json args")
    path: str | None = Field(default=None, description="primary path the call touched, if determinable")
    command: str | None = Field(default=None, description="run_command only")
    mutating: bool = False
    fault: FaultFired | None = None
    outcome: Literal["ok", "error", "short_circuit", "ack_lost"]
    exit_code: int | None = None
    duration_ms: int = 0

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- gym REST

class FileEntry(BaseModel):
    path: str
    size: int
    sha256: str
    status: Literal["added", "modified", "deleted", "unchanged"] = "unchanged"

class ResetRequest(BaseModel):
    scenario_id: str
    seed: int | None = None

class ResetResponse(BaseModel):
    episode_id: str
    scenario: Scenario
    workspace_root: str = "/workspace"
    files: list[FileEntry]
    sandbox_id: str | None = Field(default=None, description="for ops/evidence only; never given to the model")

class FileDiff(BaseModel):
    path: str
    unified: str

class ObserveResponse(BaseModel):
    episode_id: str
    step: int
    files: list[FileEntry]
    diffs: list[FileDiff] = Field(default_factory=list)
    faults_fired: list[FaultFired] = Field(default_factory=list, description="only faults that already fired; pending ones stay hidden")
    done: bool = False

class Check(BaseModel):
    id: str
    ok: bool
    weight: float = 1.0
    detail: str = ""

class TestsResult(BaseModel):
    passed: int = 0
    failed: int = 0
    errors: int = 0
    output: str = ""

class EvaluateResponse(BaseModel):
    episode_id: str
    score: float = Field(ge=0, le=100, description="60*tests_pass + 40*weighted recovery checks")
    passed: bool = Field(description="hidden tests all pass")
    checks: list[Check]
    tests: TestsResult
    ledger: list[LedgerEntry] = Field(default_factory=list, description="full ledger, released only at evaluate time")

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- harness runs & events

EventType = Literal[
    "run.started",
    "episode.reset",
    "turn.text",
    "tool.call",
    "tool.result",
    "fault.fired",
    "workspace.diff",
    "episode.evaluated",
    "run.finished",
    "log",
    "llm.call",
    "turn.thinking",
]

class Event(BaseModel):
    """Append-only, single-writer (run_episode). `id` is the 0-based sequence within the run and is
    what SSE uses as the event id / Last-Event-ID.

    data shapes by type:
      run.started       {scenario_id, model, seed, max_steps, anthropic_workspace?: masked}
      episode.reset     {episode_id, files: [FileEntry], task_prompt}
      turn.text         {text}
      tool.call         {tool, input, tool_use_id}
      tool.result       {tool_use_id, tool, output: str, is_error: bool, duration_ms, fault?: FaultFired}
      fault.fired       FaultFired
      workspace.diff    {files: [FileEntry], diffs: [FileDiff]}
      episode.evaluated EvaluateResponse
      run.finished      {status, usage: {input_tokens, output_tokens}, duration_ms, error?: str}
      log               {svc, lvl, ev, msg, ...}   (unified log line mirrored into the stream)
      llm.call          {attempt, model, stop_reason?, request_id?, usage: Usage-shaped {input_tokens, output_tokens, cache_read_input_tokens?, cache_creation_input_tokens?}, duration_ms, error?}
      turn.thinking     {text}   (summarised thinking, models that expose it)
    """

    id: int
    ts: str
    run_id: str
    type: EventType
    step: int | None = None
    data: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)

RunStatus = Literal["queued", "running", "ok", "error", "truncated"]

MODEL_ALLOWLIST = ["claude-haiku-4-5", "claude-sonnet-5", "claude-opus-5"]
DEFAULT_MODEL = "claude-haiku-4-5"

class RunRequest(BaseModel):
    scenario_id: str
    model: str | None = Field(default=None, description="must be in MODEL_ALLOWLIST; None = ANTHROPIC_MODEL env")
    seed: int | None = None
    max_steps: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=1, le=40)

class Usage(BaseModel):
    input_tokens: int = 0
    output_tokens: int = 0
    cache_read_input_tokens: int = 0
    cache_creation_input_tokens: int = 0

class RunRecord(BaseModel):
    run_id: str
    status: RunStatus
    scenario_id: str
    model: str
    seed: int | None = None
    max_steps: int = 20
    episode_id: str | None = None

    # additive (ARCHITECTURE.md §4 / PLAN.md §2.9): persistence + transcript grouping
    conversation_id: str | None = None
    user_id: str | None = None
    task_prompt: str | None = None
    steps: int | None = None
    summary: str | None = Field(default=None, description="the agent's submit summary")

Tool reply2026-09-12T21:58:29.260Z
1	/**
2	 * Typed harness client: plain cross-origin JSON fetches plus a resilient run subscription.
3	 *
4	 * Reliability model (this is the interesting part):
5	 *  - The harness closes every SSE stream at ~110 s to stay under Modal's 150 s request cap, so a
6	 *    clean close mid-run is NORMAL and must not be treated as a failure. We distinguish the two
7	 *    by whether the stream delivered anything before it closed.
8	 *  - EventSource cannot set headers, so the resume point is passed both natively (the browser
9	 *    sends `Last-Event-ID` on its own reconnect) and as a `last_event_id` query param on ours.
10	 *  - After two consecutive failed opens we stop trusting SSE and fall back to polling
11	 *    GET /runs/{id} every 1.5 s, which is strictly less efficient but works through anything.
12	 *  - No credentials are ever sent: the web origin and the harness origin are different modal.run
13	 *    subdomains and these are ordinary CORS requests.
14	 */
15	
16	import { getLogger } from './log'
17	import type { Event, EventType, Health, RunRecord, RunRequest, Scenario } from './types'
18	import { isTerminal } from './reducer'
19	
20	const log = getLogger('web')
21	
22	const EVENT_TYPES: EventType[] = [
23	  'run.started',
24	  'episode.reset',
25	  'turn.text',
26	  'tool.call',
27	  'tool.result',
28	  'fault.fired',
29	  'workspace.diff',
30	  'episode.evaluated',
31	  'run.finished',
32	  'log',
33	]
34	
35	export class HarnessError extends Error {
36	  constructor(
37	    message: string,
38	    readonly status?: number,
39	    readonly url?: string,
40	  ) {
41	    super(message)
42	    this.name = 'HarnessError'
43	  }
44	}
45	
46	async function jsonFetch<T>(
47	  url: string,
48	  init: RequestInit = {},
49	  fetchImpl: typeof fetch = fetch,
50	): Promise<T> {
51	  const t0 = Date.now()
52	  let res: Response
53	  try {
54	    res = await fetchImpl(url, {
55	      ...init,
56	      credentials: 'omit',
57	      headers: { accept: 'application/json', ...(init.headers ?? {}) },
58	    })
59	  } catch (err) {
60	    log.error('http.error', 'request failed', { url, error: String(err) })
61	    throw new HarnessError(`network error contacting ${url}: ${String(err)}`, undefined, url)
62	  }
63	  const durMs = Date.now() - t0
64	  if (!res.ok) {
65	    const body = await res.text().catch(() => '')
66	    log.error('http.response', 'non-2xx', { url, status: res.status, dur_ms: durMs })
67	    throw new HarnessError(
68	      `${res.status} ${res.statusText} from ${url}${body ? `: ${body.slice(0, 400)}` : ''}`,
69	      res.status,
70	      url,
71	    )
72	  }
73	  log.debug('http.response', 'ok', { url, status: res.status, dur_ms: durMs })
74	  return (await res.json()) as T
75	}
76	
77	/**
78	 * PLAN.md 2.3 specifies `GET /scenarios -> [Scenario]`, but the deployed harness answers
79	 * `{"scenarios": [...]}`. Rather than break on either, accept both shapes here — this is the one
80	 * place in the app that has to know, and a client that tolerates an envelope costs nothing.
81	 * Logged as a contract deviation.
82	 */
83	export function normaliseScenarios(body: unknown): Scenario[] {
84	  if (Array.isArray(body)) return body as Scenario[]
85	  if (body && typeof body === 'object') {
86	    const inner = (body as { scenarios?: unknown }).scenarios
87	    if (Array.isArray(inner)) {
88	      log.debug('scenarios.envelope', 'harness returned {scenarios: [...]}, unwrapping', {
89	        count: inner.length,
90	      })
91	      return inner as Scenario[]
92	    }
93	  }
94	  log.warn('scenarios.shape', 'unrecognised /scenarios payload; treating as empty', {
95	    got: typeof body,
96	  })
97	  return []
98	}
99	
100	export class HarnessClient {
101	  constructor(
102	    readonly base: string,
103	    private readonly fetchImpl: typeof fetch = fetch,
104	  ) {}
105	
106	  health(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<Health> {
107	    return jsonFetch<Health>(`${this.base}/health`, { signal }, this.fetchImpl)
108	  }
109	
110	  async scenarios(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<Scenario[]> {
111	    const body = await jsonFetch<Scenario[] | { scenarios?: Scenario[] }>(
112	      `${this.base}/scenarios`,
113	      { signal },
114	      this.fetchImpl,
115	    )
116	    return normaliseScenarios(body)
117	  }
118	
119	  createRun(req: RunRequest, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<{ run_id: string }> {
120	    log.info('run.create', 'POST /runs', {
121	      scenario_id: req.scenario_id,
122	      model: req.model ?? null,
123	    })
124	    return jsonFetch<{ run_id: string }>(
125	      `${this.base}/runs`,
126	      {
127	        method: 'POST',
128	        headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
129	        body: JSON.stringify(req),
130	        signal,
131	      },
132	      this.fetchImpl,
133	    )
134	  }
135	
136	  getRun(runId: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<RunRecord> {
137	    return jsonFetch<RunRecord>(
138	      `${this.base}/runs/${encodeURIComponent(runId)}`,
139	      { signal },
140	      this.fetchImpl,
141	    )
142	  }
143	}

145	// ------------------------------------------------------------------ run subscription
146	
147	export type TransportMode = 'sse' | 'poll' | 'closed'
148	
149	export interface TransportState {
150	  mode: TransportMode
151	  /** Consecutive failed SSE opens. Reset whenever a stream delivers an event. */
152	  errors: number
153	  detail?: string
154	}
155	
156	export interface SubscribeOptions {
157	  base: string
158	  runId: string
159	  /** Resume point; -1 means "from the beginning". */
160	  lastEventId?: number
161	  onEvents: (events: Event[]) => void
162	  onRecord?: (record: RunRecord) => void
163	  onTransport?: (state: TransportState) => void
164	  onDone?: () => void
165	  /** Injection points for tests. */
166	  eventSourceImpl?: typeof EventSource | null
167	  fetchImpl?: typeof fetch
168	  pollIntervalMs?: number
169	  reconnectDelayMs?: number
170	  maxSseErrors?: number
171	  setTimeoutImpl?: (fn: () => void, ms: number) => unknown
172	  clearTimeoutImpl?: (handle: unknown) => void
173	}
174	
175	export interface Subscription {
176	  close: () => void
177	  /** Test/debug introspection. */
178	  state: () => TransportState
179	}
180	
181	function parseEvent(raw: string): Event | null {
182	  try {
183	    const v = JSON.parse(raw) as Event
184	    return typeof v?.id === 'number' && typeof v?.type === 'string' ? v : null
185	  } catch {
186	    return null
187	  }
188	}
189	
190	/**
191	 * Tail a run. Returns immediately; every update arrives through the callbacks.
192	 * Always call `close()` — it tears down the EventSource and any pending timer.
193	 */
194	export function subscribeRun(opts: SubscribeOptions): Subscription {
195	  const {
196	    base,
197	    runId,
198	    onEvents,
199	    onRecord,
200	    onTransport,
201	    onDone,
202	    fetchImpl = typeof fetch !== 'undefined' ? fetch : undefined,
203	    pollIntervalMs = 1500,
204	    reconnectDelayMs = 1000,
205	    maxSseErrors = 2,
206	    setTimeoutImpl = (fn, ms) => setTimeout(fn, ms),
207	    clearTimeoutImpl = (h) => clearTimeout(h as ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>),
208	  } = opts
209	
210	  const ESImpl =
211	    opts.eventSourceImpl !== undefined
212	      ? opts.eventSourceImpl
213	      : typeof EventSource !== 'undefined'
214	        ? EventSource
215	        : null
216	
217	  let lastEventId = opts.lastEventId ?? -1
218	  let closed = false
219	  let es: EventSource | null = null
220	  let timer: unknown = null
221	  let transport: TransportState = { mode: 'sse', errors: 0 }
222	
223	  const setTransport = (patch: Partial<TransportState>) => {
224	    transport = { ...transport, ...patch }
225	    onTransport?.(transport)
226	  }
227	
228	  const clearTimer = () => {
229	    if (timer !== null) {
230	      clearTimeoutImpl(timer)
231	      timer = null
232	    }
233	  }
234	
235	  const emit = (events: Event[]) => {
236	    const fresh = events.filter((e) => e.id > lastEventId)
237	    if (fresh.length === 0) return
238	    lastEventId = Math.max(lastEventId, ...fresh.map((e) => e.id))
239	    onEvents(fresh)
240	  }
241	
242	  const finish = (reason: string) => {
243	    if (closed) return
244	    log.info('stream.done', reason, { run_id: runId, last_event_id: lastEventId })
245	    closed = true
246	    es?.close()
247	    es = null
248	    clearTimer()
249	    setTransport({ mode: 'closed', detail: reason })
250	    onDone?.()
251	  }
252	
253	  // ------------------------------------------------ polling fallback
254	
255	  const pollOnce = async () => {
256	    if (closed || !fetchImpl) return
257	    try {
258	      const rec = await jsonFetch<RunRecord>(
259	        `${base}/runs/${encodeURIComponent(runId)}`,
260	        {},
261	        fetchImpl,
262	      )
263	      onRecord?.(rec)
264	      emit(rec.events ?? [])
265	      if (isTerminal(rec.status)) {
266	        finish('run reached a terminal status (poll)')
267	        return
268	      }
269	    } catch (err) {
270	      log.warn('poll.error', 'GET /runs failed', { run_id: runId, error: String(err) })
271	      setTransport({ detail: String(err) })
272	    }
273	    if (!closed) timer = setTimeoutImpl(() => void pollOnce(), pollIntervalMs)
274	  }
275	
276	  const startPolling = (why: string) => {
277	    if (closed) return
278	    es?.close()
279	    es = null
280	    clearTimer()
281	    log.warn('stream.fallback', `switching to polling: ${why}`, {
282	      run_id: runId,
283	      interval_ms: pollIntervalMs,
284	    })
285	    setTransport({ mode: 'poll', detail: why })
286	    void pollOnce()
287	  }
288	
289	  // ------------------------------------------------ SSE
290	
291	  const openSse = () => {
292	    if (closed || !ESImpl) return
293	    const url = new URL(`${base}/runs/${encodeURIComponent(runId)}/events`)
294	    if (lastEventId >= 0) url.searchParams.set('last_event_id', String(lastEventId))
295	    let deliveredThisStream = 0
296	
297	    log.info('stream.open', 'opening SSE', { run_id: runId, url: url.toString() })
298	    const source = new ESImpl(url.toString())
299	    es = source
300	
301	    const handle = (e: MessageEvent) => {
302	      const ev = parseEvent(e.data as string)
303	      if (!ev) {
304	        log.warn('stream.parse_error', 'unparseable SSE frame', { run_id: runId })
305	        return
306	      }
307	      deliveredThisStream += 1
308	      if (transport.errors !== 0) setTransport({ errors: 0, detail: undefined })
309	      emit([ev])
310	      if (ev.type === 'run.finished') {
311	        // Let the server send `done`; if it does not, the close handler below stops us.
312	        log.info('stream.run_finished', 'run.finished seen', { run_id: runId })
313	      }
314	    }
315	
316	    source.addEventListener('message', handle as EventListener)
317	    for (const t of EVENT_TYPES) source.addEventListener(t, handle as EventListener)
318	    source.addEventListener('done', (() => {
319	      finish('server sent `event: done`')
320	    }) as EventListener)
321	
322	    source.addEventListener('open', () => {
323	      log.info('stream.connected', 'SSE open', { run_id: runId })
324	      setTransport({ mode: 'sse' })
325	    })
326	
327	    source.addEventListener('error', () => {
328	      if (closed) return
329	      source.close()
330	      if (es === source) es = null
331	      if (deliveredThisStream > 0) {
332	        // Expected: the harness rotates the stream every ~110 s. Reconnect at once.
333	        log.info('stream.rotate', 'stream closed after delivering events; reconnecting', {
334	          run_id: runId,
335	          delivered: deliveredThisStream,
336	          last_event_id: lastEventId,
337	        })
338	        setTransport({ errors: 0 })
339	        timer = setTimeoutImpl(openSse, 0)
340	        return
341	      }
342	      const errors = transport.errors + 1
343	      setTransport({ errors, detail: 'EventSource error' })
344	      log.warn('stream.error', 'SSE failed before delivering anything', {
345	        run_id: runId,
346	        errors,
347	      })
348	      if (errors >= maxSseErrors) {
349	        startPolling(`${errors} consecutive EventSource errors`)
350	        return
351	      }
352	      timer = setTimeoutImpl(openSse, reconnectDelayMs)
353	    })
354	  }
355	
356	  if (ESImpl) {
357	    openSse()
358	  } else {
359	    startPolling('EventSource unavailable in this environment')
360	  }
361	
362	  return {
363	    close: () => {
364	      if (closed) return
365	      closed = true
366	      es?.close()
367	      es = null
368	      clearTimer()
369	      setTransport({ mode: 'closed', detail: 'closed by caller' })
370	      log.info('stream.close', 'subscription closed by caller', { run_id: runId })
371	    },
372	    state: () => transport,
373	  }
374	}

1	/**
2	 * Pure fold: Event[] -> ViewState.
3	 *
4	 * Everything the UI renders comes from here, so the same function drives a live SSE run and a
5	 * bundled replay, and the interesting derivations (which call a fault hit, whether the agent
6	 * recovered) are unit-testable without a browser.
7	 *
8	 * Derivations worth naming:
9	 *   - `mutating` / `read` classification and argv-token path matching mirror
10	 *     services/sandbox-env/GRADING.md so the badge in the UI and the grader's checks agree.
11	 *   - `recovered`: a faulted call is recovered when a later call on the same path succeeds AND a
12	 *     successful *read* of that path happened at or before that success. Retrying blind does not
13	 *     count — that is precisely the behaviour the `lost-ack` scenario grades.
14	 */
15

17	  Check,
18	  ErrorCode,
19	  EvaluateResponse,
20	  Event,
21	  FaultFired,

23	  FileEntry,

36	  return !!s && TERMINAL_STATUSES.includes(s)
37	}

136	// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- path / verb helpers
137	
138	/** `"./src/x.py"`, `'/workspace/src/x.py'`, `"src/x.py"` all normalise to `src/x.py`. */
139	export function normalisePath(raw: string): string {
140	  let s = raw.trim()
141	  if (s.length >= 2 && ((s[0] === '"' && s.endsWith('"')) || (s[0] === "'" && s.endsWith("'")))) {
142	    s = s.slice(1, -1)
143	  }
144	  s = s.replace(/^\/workspace\//, '')
145	  s = s.replace(/^\.\//, '')
146	  s = s.replace(/^\/+/, '')
147	  // `ls src/ratelimiter/` and `read_file src/ratelimiter` name the same thing.
148	  if (s.length > 1) s = s.replace(/\/+$/, '')
149	  return s
150	}
151	
152	/** Cheap argv split: whitespace, honouring single/double quotes. Good enough for token matching. */
153	export function argvTokens(command: string): string[] {
154	  const out: string[] = []
155	  const re = /"([^"]*)"|'([^']*)'|(\S+)/g
156	  let m: RegExpExecArray | null
157	  while ((m = re.exec(command)) !== null) {
158	    out.push(m[1] ?? m[2] ?? m[3] ?? '')
159	  }
160	  return out
161	}
162	
163	const MUTATING_RE =
164	  /(>>?|\btee\b|\bsed\s+-i|\brm\b|\bmv\b|\bcp\b|\btouch\b|\bmkdir\b|\btruncate\b|\bpatch\b|\bgit\s+(apply|checkout|reset|restore))/
165	
166	const PY_WRITE_RE = /python[0-9.]*\s+.*-c[\s\S]*open\([\s\S]*['"][wa]\+?['"]/
167	
168	const READ_VERB_RE =
169	  /\b(cat|head|tail|grep|rg|wc|diff|less|cmp|sha256sum|md5sum|ls|stat|find|pytest|nl|od)\b|\bsed\s+-n\b|python[0-9.]*\s+-m\s+pytest/
170	
171	export function commandIsMutating(command: string): boolean {
172	  return MUTATING_RE.test(command) || PY_WRITE_RE.test(command)
173	}
174	
175	export function commandIsRead(command: string): boolean {
176	  return !commandIsMutating(command) && READ_VERB_RE.test(command)
177	}
178	
179	/** Does this call touch `path`? Direct for path tools; argv-token match for run_command. */
180	export function callTouches(call: ToolCallView, path: string): boolean {
181	  const target = normalisePath(path)
182	  if (call.tool === 'run_command') {
183	    if (!call.command) return false
184	    return argvTokens(call.command).some((t) => {
185	      const n = normalisePath(t)
186	      return n === target || n.endsWith(`/${target}`)
187	    })
188	  }
189	  return call.path !== null && call.path === target
190	}
191	
192	function inputPath(tool: string, input: Record<string, unknown>): string | null {
193	  const raw = input?.path
194	  if (typeof raw === 'string' && raw.length > 0) return normalisePath(raw)
195	  if (tool === 'list_dir') return '.'
196	  return null
197	}
198	
199	function classify(
200	  tool: string,
201	  input: Record<string, unknown>,
202	): { path: string | null; command: string | null; mutating: boolean; read: boolean } {
203	  if (tool === 'run_command') {
204	    const command = typeof input?.command === 'string' ? (input.command as string) : null
205	    return {
206	      path: null,
207	      command,
208	      mutating: command ? commandIsMutating(command) : false,
209	      read: command ? commandIsRead(command) : false,
210	    }
211	  }
212	  if (tool === 'write_file') {
213	    return { path: inputPath(tool, input), command: null, mutating: true, read: false }
214	  }
215	  if (tool === 'read_file' || tool === 'list_dir') {
216	    return { path: inputPath(tool, input), command: null, mutating: false, read: true }
217	  }
218	  // `submit` and anything else the harness adds locally.
219	  return { path: inputPath(tool, input), command: null, mutating: false, read: false }
220	}
221	
222	// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- result parsing
223	
224	const ERROR_CODES: ErrorCode[] = ['ENOENT', 'EACCES', 'ETIMEDOUT', 'EINVAL', 'EINTERNAL', 'ENOEPISODE']
225	
226	export function parseToolResult(
227	  toolUseId: string,
228	  output: string,
229	  isError: boolean,
230	  durationMs: number,
231	): ToolResultView {
232	  const base: ToolResultView = {
233	    toolUseId,
234	    output: output ?? '',
235	    isError,
236	    durationMs,
237	    stdout: null,
238	    stderr: null,
239	    content: null,
240	    exitCode: null,
241	    errorText: null,
242	    errorCode: null,
243	    truncated: false,
244	    bytesWritten: null,
245	    entries: null,
246	  }
247	  let parsed: unknown
248	  try {
249	    parsed = JSON.parse(output)
250	  } catch {
251	    if (isError) base.errorText = output
252	    return base
253	  }
254	  if (parsed === null || typeof parsed !== 'object' || Array.isArray(parsed)) return base
255	  const o = parsed as Record<string, unknown>
256	  if (typeof o.stdout === 'string') base.stdout = o.stdout
257	  if (typeof o.stderr === 'string') base.stderr = o.stderr
258	  if (typeof o.content === 'string') base.content = o.content
259	  if (typeof o.exit_code === 'number') base.exitCode = o.exit_code
260	  if (typeof o.truncated === 'boolean') base.truncated = o.truncated
261	  if (typeof o.bytes_written === 'number') base.bytesWritten = o.bytes_written
262	  if (typeof o.error === 'string') base.errorText = o.error
263	  if (typeof o.code === 'string' && (ERROR_CODES as string[]).includes(o.code)) {
264	    base.errorCode = o.code as ErrorCode
265	  }
266	  if (Array.isArray(o.entries)) {
267	    base.entries = o.entries as ToolResultView['entries']
268	  }
269	  return base
270	}
271	
272	// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- recovery
273	
274	function flatten(steps: StepView[]): ToolCallView[] {
275	  const all: ToolCallView[] = []
276	  for (const s of steps) all.push(...s.calls)
277	  all.sort((a, b) => a.seq - b.seq)
278	  return all
279	}
280	
281	/**
282	 * Recompute `recovered` for every faulted call. Cheap (a run is tens of calls) and keeps the flag
283	 * correct as later events arrive.
284	 */
285	export function applyRecovery(steps: StepView[]): StepView[] {
286	  const all = flatten(steps)
287	  const recoveredIds = new Set<string>()
288	
289	  for (const c of all) {
290	    if (!c.fault) continue
291	    const path = normalisePath(c.fault.path || c.path || '')
292	    if (!path) continue
293	    const later = all.filter((x) => x.seq > c.seq)
294	    const successes = later.filter((x) => x.result && !x.result.isError && callTouches(x, path))
295	    if (successes.length === 0) continue
296	    const firstSuccessSeq = successes[0]!.seq
297	    const verified = later.some(
298	      (x) => x.seq <= firstSuccessSeq && x.read && x.result && !x.result.isError && callTouches(x, path),
299	    )
300	    if (verified) recoveredIds.add(c.toolUseId)
301	  }
302	
303	  if (recoveredIds.size === 0) {
304	    return steps.map((s) =>
305	      s.calls.some((c) => c.recovered) ? { ...s, calls: s.calls.map((c) => ({ ...c, recovered: false })) } : s,
306	    )
307	  }
308	  return steps.map((s) => ({
309	    ...s,
310	    calls: s.calls.map((c) => {
311	      const next = recoveredIds.has(c.toolUseId)
312	      return c.recovered === next ? c : { ...c, recovered: next }
313	    }),
314	  }))
315	}
316	
317	// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- the fold
318	
319	function upsertStep(steps: StepView[], step: number): { steps: StepView[]; idx: number } {
320	  const idx = steps.findIndex((s) => s.step === step)
321	  if (idx >= 0) return { steps, idx }
322	  const next = [...steps, { step, texts: [], calls: [] }].sort((a, b) => a.step - b.step)
323	  return { steps: next, idx: next.findIndex((s) => s.step === step) }
324	}
325	
326	function asRecord(v: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
327	  return v !== null && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v) ? (v as Record<string, unknown>) : {}
328	}
329	
330	function num(v: unknown, fallback: number): number {
331	  return typeof v === 'number' && Number.isFinite(v) ? v : fallback
332	}
333	
334	function str(v: unknown): string | null {
335	  return typeof v === 'string' ? v : null
336	}
337	
338	function asFault(v: unknown): FaultFired | null {
339	  const o = asRecord(v)
340	  if (typeof o.kind !== 'string' || typeof o.path !== 'string') return null
341	  return {
342	    step: num(o.step, 0),
343	    kind: o.kind as FaultFired['kind'],
344	    path: o.path,
345	    mode: (o.mode as FaultFired['mode']) ?? 'transient',
346	  }
347	}
348	
349	function nextSeq(steps: StepView[]): number {
350	  let max = -1
351	  for (const s of steps) for (const c of s.calls) if (c.seq > max) max = c.seq
352	  return max + 1
353	}
354	
355	/** Fold one event into the view state. Pure: never mutates `state`. */
356	export function reduce(state: ViewState, ev: Event): ViewState {
357	  if (!ev || typeof ev.type !== 'string') return state
358	  const data = asRecord(ev.data)
359	  const step = typeof ev.step === 'number' ? ev.step : state.step
360	  const next: ViewState = {
361	    ...state,
362	    runId: state.runId ?? str(ev.run_id),
363	    lastEventId: typeof ev.id === 'number' && ev.id > state.lastEventId ? ev.id : state.lastEventId,
364	    eventCount: state.eventCount + 1,
365	    step: Math.max(state.step, typeof ev.step === 'number' ? ev.step : 0),
366	  }
367	
368	  switch (ev.type) {
369	    case 'run.started': {
370	      next.status = 'running'
371	      next.scenarioId = str(data.scenario_id) ?? next.scenarioId
372	      next.model = str(data.model) ?? next.model
373	      next.seed = typeof data.seed === 'number' ? data.seed : next.seed
374	      next.maxSteps = num(data.max_steps, next.maxSteps)
375	      next.anthropicWorkspace = str(data.anthropic_workspace) ?? next.anthropicWorkspace
376	      next.startedAt = state.startedAt ?? str(ev.ts)
377	      return next
378	    }
379	
380	    case 'episode.reset': {
381	      next.episodeId = str(data.episode_id) ?? next.episodeId
382	      next.taskPrompt = str(data.task_prompt) ?? next.taskPrompt
383	      if (Array.isArray(data.files)) next.files = data.files as FileEntry[]
384	      if (next.status === 'queued') next.status = 'running'
385	      return next
386	    }
387	
388	    case 'turn.text': {
389	      const text = str(data.text)
390	      if (!text || text.trim().length === 0) return next
391	      const { steps, idx } = upsertStep(state.steps, step)
392	      const target = steps[idx]!
393	      const updated = [...steps]
394	      updated[idx] = { ...target, texts: [...target.texts, text] }
395	      next.steps = updated
396	      return next
397	    }
398	
399	    case 'tool.call': {
400	      const tool = str(data.tool) ?? 'unknown'
401	      const toolUseId = str(data.tool_use_id) ?? `${ev.id}`
402	      const input = asRecord(data.input)
403	      const { steps, idx } = upsertStep(state.steps, step)
404	      const target = steps[idx]!
405	      if (target.calls.some((c) => c.toolUseId === toolUseId)) {
406	        next.steps = steps
407	        return next
408	      }
409	      const cls = classify(tool, input)
410	      const call: ToolCallView = {
411	        seq: nextSeq(steps),
412	        step,
413	        toolUseId,
414	        tool,
415	        input,
416	        path: cls.path,
417	        command: cls.command,
418	        mutating: cls.mutating,
419	        read: cls.read,
420	        result: null,
421	        fault: null,
422	        recovered: false,
423	      }
424	      const updated = [...steps]
425	      updated[idx] = { ...target, calls: [...target.calls, call] }
426	      next.steps = applyRecovery(updated)
427	      return next
428	    }
429	
430	    case 'tool.result': {
431	      const toolUseId = str(data.tool_use_id)
432	      if (!toolUseId) return next
433	      const isError = data.is_error === true
434	      const result = parseToolResult(
435	        toolUseId,
436	        str(data.output) ?? '',
437	        isError,
438	        num(data.duration_ms, 0),
439	      )
440	      const fault = asFault(data.fault)
441	      let found = false
442	      const updated = state.steps.map((s) => {
443	        if (!s.calls.some((c) => c.toolUseId === toolUseId)) return s
444	        found = true
445	        return {
446	          ...s,
447	          calls: s.calls.map((c) =>
448	            c.toolUseId === toolUseId ? { ...c, result, fault: fault ?? c.fault } : c,
449	          ),
450	        }
451	      })
452	      if (!found) {
453	        // A result without its call (resumed mid-run, or events dropped): synthesise the call so
454	        // the timeline still shows the output rather than silently losing it.
455	        const tool = str(data.tool) ?? 'unknown'
456	        const { steps, idx } = upsertStep(state.steps, step)
457	        const target = steps[idx]!
458	        const cls = classify(tool, {})
459	        const synth: ToolCallView = {
460	          seq: nextSeq(steps),
461	          step,
462	          toolUseId,
463	          tool,
464	          input: {},
465	          path: cls.path,
466	          command: cls.command,
467	          mutating: cls.mutating,
468	          read: cls.read,
469	          result,
470	          fault,
471	          recovered: false,
472	        }
473	        const s2 = [...steps]
474	        s2[idx] = { ...target, calls: [...target.calls, synth] }
475	        next.steps = applyRecovery(s2)
476	        return next
477	      }
478	      if (fault && !state.faults.some((f) => f.step === fault.step && f.path === fault.path && f.kind === fault.kind)) {
479	        next.faults = [...state.faults, fault]
480	      }
481	      next.steps = applyRecovery(updated)
482	      return next
483	    }
484	
485	    case 'fault.fired': {
486	      const fault = asFault(data) ?? asFault(data.fault)
487	      if (!fault) return next
488	      const known = state.faults.some(
489	        (f) => f.step === fault.step && f.path === fault.path && f.kind === fault.kind,
490	      )
491	      next.faults = known ? state.faults : [...state.faults, fault]
492	      // Attach to the call at that step that touched the path and has no fault yet.
493	      const updated = state.steps.map((s) => {
494	        if (s.step !== fault.step) return s
495	        let attached = false
496	        const calls = s.calls.map((c) => {
497	          if (attached || c.fault) return c
498	          if (!callTouches(c, fault.path) && c.path !== normalisePath(fault.path)) return c
499	          attached = true
500	          return { ...c, fault }
501	        })
502	        return attached ? { ...s, calls } : s
503	      })
504	      next.steps = applyRecovery(updated)
505	      return next
506	    }
507	
508	    case 'workspace.diff': {
509	      if (Array.isArray(data.files)) next.files = data.files as FileEntry[]
510	      if (Array.isArray(data.diffs)) next.diffs = data.diffs as FileDiff[]
511	      return next
512	    }
513	
514	    case 'episode.evaluated': {
515	      const checks = Array.isArray(data.checks) ? (data.checks as Check[]) : []
516	      next.evaluation = {
517	        episode_id: str(data.episode_id) ?? state.episodeId ?? '',
518	        score: num(data.score, 0),
519	        passed: data.passed === true,
520	        checks,
521	        tests: asRecord(data.tests) as unknown as EvaluateResponse['tests'],
522	        ledger: Array.isArray(data.ledger) ? (data.ledger as EvaluateResponse['ledger']) : [],
523	      }
524	      return next
525	    }
526	
527	    case 'run.finished': {
528	      const status = str(data.status)
529	      next.status = (status as RunStatus) ?? 'ok'
530	      const usage = asRecord(data.usage)
531	      next.usage = {
532	        input_tokens: num(usage.input_tokens, state.usage.input_tokens),
533	        output_tokens: num(usage.output_tokens, state.usage.output_tokens),
534	      }
535	      next.durationMs = typeof data.duration_ms === 'number' ? data.duration_ms : state.durationMs
536	      next.error = str(data.error) ?? state.error
537	      next.finishedAt = str(ev.ts) ?? state.finishedAt
538	      return next
539	    }
540	
541	    case 'log': {
542	      const line = data as unknown as LogLine
543	      if (typeof line?.ev !== 'string') return next
544	      next.logs = [...state.logs, line].slice(-LOG_BUFFER_LIMIT)
545	      return next
546	    }
547	
548	    default:
549	      return next
550	  }
551	}
552	
553	export function reduceAll(events: Event[], from: ViewState = initialState()): ViewState {
554	  return events.reduce(reduce, from)
555	}
556	
557	/**
558	 * Seed the view from a whole RunRecord (page refresh mid-run, or a bundled demo). Record-level
559	 * fields win over anything the events implied, because the record is the harness's own summary.
560	 */
561	export function fromRunRecord(rec: RunRecord): ViewState {
562	  const folded = reduceAll(rec.events ?? [])
563	  return {
564	    ...folded,
565	    runId: rec.run_id ?? folded.runId,
566	    status: rec.status ?? folded.status,
567	    scenarioId: rec.scenario_id ?? folded.scenarioId,
568	    model: rec.model ?? folded.model,
569	    seed: rec.seed ?? folded.seed,
570	    maxSteps: rec.max_steps ?? folded.maxSteps,
571	    episodeId: rec.episode_id ?? folded.episodeId,
572	    startedAt: rec.created_at ?? folded.startedAt,
573	    finishedAt: rec.finished_at ?? folded.finishedAt,
574	    evaluation: rec.evaluation ?? folded.evaluation,
575	    usage: rec.usage ?? folded.usage,
576	    error: rec.error ?? folded.error,
577	  }
578	}
579	
580	// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- selectors
581	
582	export function allCalls(state: ViewState): ToolCallView[] {
583	  return flatten(state.steps)
584	}
585	
586	export function faultCount(state: ViewState): number {
587	  return allCalls(state).filter((c) => c.fault).length
588	}
589	
590	export function recoveredCount(state: ViewState): number {
591	  return allCalls(state).filter((c) => c.fault && c.recovered).length
592	}
593	
594	export function elapsedMs(state: ViewState, now: number = Date.now()): number | null {
595	  if (state.durationMs !== null) return state.durationMs
596	  if (!state.startedAt) return null
597	  const t0 = Date.parse(state.startedAt)
598	  if (Number.isNaN(t0)) return null
599	  const end = state.finishedAt ? Date.parse(state.finishedAt) : now
600	  return Math.max(0, (Number.isNaN(end) ? now : end) - t0)
601	}

Tool reply2026-09-12T21:58:30.508Z
1	/**
2	 * Unified JSON-lines logging for the browser — same shape as
3	 * packages/common/faultline_common/log.py, with svc:"web".
4	 *
5	 *   {"ts":ISO8601Z,"svc":"web","lvl":"info","run_id"?:str,"episode_id"?:str,"step"?:int,
6	 *    "ev":"dotted.event.name","msg":str, ...extras}
7	 *
8	 * Lines go to the console as a single JSON string (so a reviewer can copy/paste them next to the
9	 * Modal logs) AND into a bounded in-memory ring buffer that the Logs tab renders.
10	 */
11	
12	import type { LogLevel, LogLine } from './types'
13	
14	const LEVELS: Record<LogLevel, number> = { debug: 10, info: 20, warn: 30, error: 40 }
15	
16	const BUFFER_LIMIT = 2000
17	
18	let minLevel: number = LEVELS.info
19	let buffer: LogLine[] = []
20	let seq = 0
21	const subscribers = new Set<(lines: LogLine[]) => void>()
22	
23	export function setLogLevel(lvl: LogLevel): void {
24	  minLevel = LEVELS[lvl] ?? LEVELS.info
25	}
26	
27	export function nowIso(): string {
28	  return new Date().toISOString().replace(/(\.\d{3})\d*Z$/, '$1Z')
29	}
30	
31	/** Bound a string for log payloads, marking truncation explicitly (mirrors log.py truncate). */
32	export function truncate(s: unknown, limit = 2000): unknown {
33	  if (typeof s !== 'string' || s.length <= limit) return s
34	  return `${s.slice(0, limit)}…[truncated ${s.length - limit} chars]`
35	}
36	
37	function notify(): void {
38	  const snapshot = buffer
39	  for (const fn of subscribers) fn(snapshot)
40	}
41	
42	/** Append an already-formed unified log line (e.g. a `log` event mirrored from the harness). */
43	export function ingest(line: LogLine): void {
44	  buffer = [...buffer, { ...line, _seq: seq++ }].slice(-BUFFER_LIMIT)
45	  notify()
46	}
47	
48	export function ingestMany(lines: LogLine[]): void {
49	  if (lines.length === 0) return
50	  const stamped = lines.map((l) => ({ ...l, _seq: seq++ }))
51	  buffer = [...buffer, ...stamped].slice(-BUFFER_LIMIT)
52	  notify()
53	}
54	
55	export function getLogLines(): LogLine[] {
56	  return buffer
57	}
58	
59	export function clearLogLines(): void {
60	  buffer = []
61	  notify()
62	}
63	
64	export function subscribeLogs(fn: (lines: LogLine[]) => void): () => void {
65	  subscribers.add(fn)
66	  fn(buffer)
67	  return () => {
68	    subscribers.delete(fn)
69	  }
70	}
71	
72	const CONSOLE: Record<LogLevel, (msg: string) => void> = {
73	  debug: (m) => console.debug(m),
74	  info: (m) => console.info(m),
75	  warn: (m) => console.warn(m),
76	  error: (m) => console.error(m),
77	}
78	
79	export function logEvent(
80	  svc: string,
81	  ev: string,
82	  msg = '',
83	  lvl: LogLevel = 'info',
84	  extras: Record<string, unknown> = {},
85	): LogLine | null {
86	  if (LEVELS[lvl] < minLevel) return null
87	  const rec: LogLine = { ts: nowIso(), svc, lvl, ev, msg }
88	  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(extras)) {
89	    if (v !== undefined && v !== null) rec[k] = v
90	  }
91	  try {
92	    CONSOLE[lvl](JSON.stringify(rec))
93	  } catch {
94	    // JSON.stringify can throw on cyclic extras; never let logging break the app.
95	    CONSOLE[lvl](`{"ts":"${rec.ts}","svc":"${svc}","lvl":"${lvl}","ev":"${ev}","msg":"${msg}"}`)
96	  }
97	  ingest(rec)
98	  return rec
99	}
100	
101	export class Logger {
102	  constructor(private readonly svc: string) {}
103	
104	  debug = (ev: string, msg = '', extras: Record<string, unknown> = {}) =>
105	    logEvent(this.svc, ev, msg, 'debug', extras)
106	
107	  info = (ev: string, msg = '', extras: Record<string, unknown> = {}) =>
108	    logEvent(this.svc, ev, msg, 'info', extras)
109	
110	  warn = (ev: string, msg = '', extras: Record<string, unknown> = {}) =>
111	    logEvent(this.svc, ev, msg, 'warn', extras)
112	
113	  error = (ev: string, msg = '', extras: Record<string, unknown> = {}) =>
114	    logEvent(this.svc, ev, msg, 'error', extras)
115	}
116	
117	export function getLogger(svc: string): Logger {
118	  return new Logger(svc)
119	}

1	/**
2	 * Resolve the harness URL once, then keep the two things Home needs from it: `/health` and
3	 * `/scenarios`.
4	 *
5	 * Both are allowed to fail. A dead harness must still leave a usable page — the bundled replay
6	 * is the whole reason demo mode exists — so failures land in `healthError` / `scenariosError`
7	 * rather than throwing.
8	 */
9	
10	import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
11	
12	import { HarnessClient } from '@/lib/api'
13	import { resolveConfig, type ResolvedConfig } from '@/lib/config'
14	import { getLogger } from '@/lib/log'
15	import type { Health, Scenario } from '@/lib/types'
16	
17	const log = getLogger('web')
18	
19	export interface HarnessState {
20	  /** Resolved harness origin, or null while resolving. */
21	  base: string | null
22	  /** `/health` answered and reported ok — the only gate on starting a live run. */
23	  reachable: boolean
24	  /** Where the URL came from: window | config.json | build-env | default. */
25	  source: ResolvedConfig['source'] | null
26	  health: Health | null
27	  healthError: string | null
28	  scenarios: Scenario[]
29	  scenariosError: string | null
30	  loading: boolean
31	  refresh: () => void
32	  client: HarnessClient | null
33	}
34	
35	export function useHarness(): HarnessState {
36	  const [cfg, setCfg] = useState<ResolvedConfig | null>(null)
37	  const [health, setHealth] = useState<Health | null>(null)
38	  const [healthError, setHealthError] = useState<string | null>(null)
39	  const [scenarios, setScenarios] = useState<Scenario[]>([])
40	  const [scenariosError, setScenariosError] = useState<string | null>(null)
41	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
42	  const [nonce, setNonce] = useState(0)
43	
44	  useEffect(() => {
45	    let cancelled = false
46	    setLoading(true)
47	
48	    void (async () => {
49	      const resolved = await resolveConfig()
50	      if (cancelled) return
51	      setCfg(resolved)
52	      log.info('config.resolved', 'harness URL resolved', {
53	        harness_url: resolved.harnessUrl,
54	        source: resolved.source,
55	      })
56	      const client = new HarnessClient(resolved.harnessUrl)
57	
58	      // Deliberately NOT `await Promise.all*`: a cold harness answers /health in ~12 s while
59	      // /scenarios comes back in 0.3 s (or the other way round). Coupling them would leave the
60	      // whole page on a spinner for the slower of the two, so each settles into the UI on its own.
61	      const probeHealth = client
62	        .health()
63	        .then((value) => {
64	          if (cancelled) return
65	          setHealth(value)
66	          setHealthError(null)
67	          log.info('health.ok', 'harness reachable', {
68	            model_default: value.model_default ?? null,
69	            has_provider_key: value.has_provider_key,
70	          })
71	        })
72	        .catch((err: unknown) => {
73	          if (cancelled) return
74	          setHealth(null)
75	          setHealthError(String(err))
76	          log.warn('health.error', 'harness unreachable', { error: String(err) })
77	        })
78	
79	      const probeScenarios = client
80	        .scenarios()
81	        .then((value) => {
82	          if (cancelled) return
83	          setScenarios(value)
84	          setScenariosError(null)
85	          log.info('scenarios.loaded', 'catalogue loaded', { count: value.length })
86	        })
87	        .catch((err: unknown) => {
88	          if (cancelled) return
89	          setScenarios([])
90	          setScenariosError(String(err))
91	          log.warn('scenarios.error', 'GET /scenarios failed', { error: String(err) })
92	        })
93	
94	      await Promise.all([probeHealth, probeScenarios])
95	      if (cancelled) return
96	      setLoading(false)
97	    })()
98	
99	    return () => {
100	      cancelled = true
101	    }
102	  }, [nonce])
103	
104	  const refresh = useCallback(() => setNonce((n) => n + 1), [])
105	
106	  const client = useMemo(
107	    () => (cfg ? new HarnessClient(cfg.harnessUrl) : null),
108	    [cfg],
109	  )
110	
111	  return {
112	    base: cfg?.harnessUrl ?? null,
113	    reachable: health?.ok === true,
114	    source: cfg?.source ?? null,
115	    health,
116	    healthError,
117	    scenarios,
118	    scenariosError,
119	    loading,
120	    refresh,
121	    client,
122	  }
123	}

14	  }

23	  addEventListener(type: string, fn: Listener): void {

32	
33	  emit(type: string, data?: string): void {
34	    for (const fn of [...(this.listeners.get(type) ?? [])]) fn({ data })
35	  }
36	
37	  deliver(ev: Event): void {
38	    this.emit('message', JSON.stringify(ev))
39	  }
40	
41	  static get last(): FakeEventSource {
42	    const l = FakeEventSource.instances.at(-1)
43	    if (!l) throw new Error('no EventSource was opened')
44	    return l
45	  }
46	}
47	
48	/** Deterministic timer queue so reconnect/poll scheduling is observable without real time. */
49	function makeClock() {
50	  let nextId = 1
51	  const q: { id: number; fn: () => void }[] = []
52	  return {
53	    setTimeoutImpl: (fn: () => void, _ms: number) => {
54	      const id = nextId++
55	      q.push({ id, fn })
56	      return id
57	    },
58	    clearTimeoutImpl: (h: unknown) => {
59	      const i = q.findIndex((t) => t.id === h)
60	      if (i >= 0) q.splice(i, 1)
61	    },
62	    pending: () => q.length,
63	    /** Run exactly one queued callback, then let microtasks settle. */
64	    async tick() {
65	      const t = q.shift()
66	      if (!t) return false
67	      t.fn()
68	      for (let i = 0; i < 50; i += 1) await Promise.resolve()
69	      return true
70	    },
71	  }
72	}
73	
74	/** Let every pending microtask (async fetch chains) settle. */
75	async function settle(turns = 50): Promise<void> {
76	  for (let i = 0; i < turns; i += 1) await Promise.resolve()
77	}
78	
79	function mkEvent(id: number, type: Event['type'] = 'turn.text', data: Record<string, unknown> = { text: 'hi' }): Event {
80	  return { id, ts: '2026-09-12T14:00:00.000Z', run_id: 'r_1', type, step: 1, data }
81	}
82	
83	function jsonResponse(body: unknown, ok = true, status = 200) {
84	  return {
85	    ok,
86	    status,
87	    statusText: ok ? 'OK' : 'ERR',
88	    json: async () => body,
89	    text: async () => JSON.stringify(body),
90	  } as unknown as Response
91	}
92	
93	function record(partial: Partial<RunRecord> = {}): RunRecord {
94	  return {
95	    run_id: 'r_1',
96	    status: 'running',
97	    scenario_id: 'lost-ack',
98	    model: 'claude-haiku-4-5',
99	    max_steps: 20,
100	    created_at: '2026-09-12T14:00:00.000Z',
101	    events: [],
102	    usage: { input_tokens: 0, output_tokens: 0 },
103	    ...partial,
104	  }
105	}

168	    FakeEventSource.last.deliver(mkEvent(1))
169	    FakeEventSource.last.deliver(mkEvent(1)) // replay after a reconnect
170	    FakeEventSource.last.emit('message', 'not json')

172	    expect(onEvents).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2)
173	    expect(onEvents.mock.calls.map((c) => (c[0] as Event[])[0]!.id)).toEqual([0, 1])
174	    sub.close()

176	  })

178	  it('opens without last_event_id, and resumes with it after the ~110 s rotation', async () => {
179	    const clock = makeClock()
180	    const transports: TransportState[] = []
181	    subscribeRun({
182	      base: BASE,
183	      runId: 'r_1',
184	      onEvents: () => {},
185	      onTransport: (t) => transports.push({ ...t }),
186	      eventSourceImpl: FakeEventSource as unknown as typeof EventSource,
187	      ...clock,
188	    })
189	
190	    expect(FakeEventSource.instances).toHaveLength(1)
191	    expect(FakeEventSource.last.url).toBe(`${BASE}/runs/r_1/events`)
192	
193	    // The stream delivered something, then the server closed it: that is a rotation, not a failure.
194	    FakeEventSource.last.deliver(mkEvent(7))
195	    FakeEventSource.last.emit('error')
196	    expect(FakeEventSource.instances[0]!.closed).toBe(true)
197	
198	    await clock.tick()
199	    expect(FakeEventSource.instances).toHaveLength(2)
200	    expect(FakeEventSource.last.url).toBe(`${BASE}/runs/r_1/events?last_event_id=7`)
201	    expect(transports.every((t) => t.mode !== 'poll')).toBe(true)
202	  })
203	
204	  it('honours an explicit lastEventId on the first open', () => {
205	    const clock = makeClock()
206	    subscribeRun({
207	      base: BASE,
208	      runId: 'r_1',
209	      lastEventId: 12,
210	      onEvents: () => {},
211	      eventSourceImpl: FakeEventSource as unknown as typeof EventSource,
212	      ...clock,
213	    })
214	    expect(FakeEventSource.last.url).toBe(`${BASE}/runs/r_1/events?last_event_id=12`)
215	  })
216	
217	  it('stops on `event: done` and reports it once', () => {
218	    const onDone = vi.fn()
219	    const clock = makeClock()
220	    subscribeRun({
221	      base: BASE,
222	      runId: 'r_1',
223	      onEvents: () => {},
224	      onDone,
225	      eventSourceImpl: FakeEventSource as unknown as typeof EventSource,
226	      ...clock,
227	    })
228	    FakeEventSource.last.emit('done')
229	    FakeEventSource.last.emit('done')
230	    expect(onDone).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
231	    expect(clock.pending()).toBe(0)
232	  })
233	
234	  it('retries once, then falls back to polling after two failures with no progress', async () => {
235	    const onEvents = vi.fn()
236	    const onDone = vi.fn()
237	    const transports: TransportState[] = []
238	    const clock = makeClock()
239	    const fetchImpl = vi.fn(async () =>
240	      jsonResponse(record({ status: 'ok', events: [mkEvent(0), mkEvent(1)] })),
241	    ) as unknown as typeof fetch
242	
243	    subscribeRun({
244	      base: BASE,
245	      runId: 'r_1',
246	      onEvents,
247	      onDone,
248	      onTransport: (t) => transports.push({ ...t }),
249	      eventSourceImpl: FakeEventSource as unknown as typeof EventSource,
250	      fetchImpl,
251	      ...clock,
252	    })
253	
254	    // First failure, nothing delivered -> one retry is scheduled.
255	    FakeEventSource.last.emit('error')
256	    expect(transports.at(-1)!.errors).toBe(1)
257	    await clock.tick()
258	    expect(FakeEventSource.instances).toHaveLength(2)
259	
260	    // Second failure, still nothing delivered -> demote to polling.
261	    FakeEventSource.last.emit('error')
262	    await settle()
263	    expect(transports.some((t) => t.mode === 'poll')).toBe(true)
264	    expect(fetchImpl).toHaveBeenCalled()
265	    expect(onEvents).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
266	    expect((onEvents.mock.calls[0]![0] as Event[]).map((e) => e.id)).toEqual([0, 1])
267	    // The polled record was terminal, so the subscription closed itself.
268	    expect(onDone).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
269	    expect(transports.at(-1)!.mode).toBe('closed')
270	  })
271	
272	  it('keeps polling while the run is not terminal', async () => {
273	    const onEvents = vi.fn()
274	    const onDone = vi.fn()
275	    const clock = makeClock()
276	    let nth = 0
277	    const fetchImpl = vi.fn(async () => {
278	      nth += 1
279	      return nth === 1
280	        ? jsonResponse(record({ status: 'running', events: [mkEvent(0)] }))
281	        : jsonResponse(record({ status: 'ok', events: [mkEvent(0), mkEvent(1)] }))
282	    }) as unknown as typeof fetch
283	
284	    subscribeRun({
285	      base: BASE,
286	      runId: 'r_1',
287	      onEvents,
288	      onDone,
289	      eventSourceImpl: null, // no EventSource in this environment -> straight to polling
290	      fetchImpl,
291	      ...clock,
292	    })
293	
294	    await settle()
295	    expect(onEvents).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
296	    expect(onDone).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
297	    expect(clock.pending()).toBe(1) // the next poll is scheduled
298	
299	    await clock.tick()
300	    expect((onEvents.mock.calls[1]![0] as Event[]).map((e) => e.id)).toEqual([1])
301	    expect(onDone).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
302	  })
303	
304	  it('survives a failing poll and schedules another', async () => {
305	    const clock = makeClock()
306	    const fetchImpl = vi.fn(async () => {
307	      throw new Error('offline')
308	    }) as unknown as typeof fetch
309	
310	    const sub = subscribeRun({
311	      base: BASE,
312	      runId: 'r_1',
313	      onEvents: () => {},
314	      eventSourceImpl: null,
315	      fetchImpl,
316	      ...clock,
317	    })
318	    await settle()
319	    expect(clock.pending()).toBe(1)
320	    expect(sub.state().mode).toBe('poll')
321	    sub.close()
322	    expect(clock.pending()).toBe(0)
323	  })
324	})

258	      result(2, 'tu_r', { path: 'CHANGELOG.md', content: 'v2', size: 2, sha256: 'x' }),
259	    ])
260	    expect(recoveredCount(s)).toBe(1)
261	  })
262	
263	  it('does NOT mark a blind retry as recovered', () => {
264	    reset()
265	    const s = reduceAll([
266	      call(1, 'write_file', { path: 'src/limits.py', content: 'a' }, 'tu_1'),
267	      result(1, 'tu_1', { error: 'Permission denied', code: 'EACCES' }, true, {
268	        step: 1,
269	        kind: 'denied_write',
270	        path: 'src/limits.py',
271	        mode: 'transient',
272	      }),
273	      // straight back to writing, with no verification read in between
274	      call(2, 'write_file', { path: 'src/limits.py', content: 'a' }, 'tu_2'),
275	      result(2, 'tu_2', { path: 'src/limits.py', bytes_written: 1, sha256: 'y' }),
276	    ])
277	    expect(faultCount(s)).toBe(1)
278	    expect(recoveredCount(s)).toBe(0)
279	  })
280	
281	  it('marks denied_write recovered when a read precedes the successful retry', () => {
282	    reset()
283	    const s = reduceAll([
284	      call(1, 'write_file', { path: 'src/limits.py', content: 'a' }, 'tu_1'),
285	      result(1, 'tu_1', { error: 'Permission denied', code: 'EACCES' }, true, {
286	        step: 1,
287	        kind: 'denied_write',
288	        path: 'src/limits.py',
289	        mode: 'transient',
290	      }),
291	      call(2, 'run_command', { command: 'cat src/limits.py' }, 'tu_2'),
292	      result(2, 'tu_2', { stdout: 'old', stderr: '', exit_code: 0, duration_ms: 4 }),
293	      call(3, 'write_file', { path: 'src/limits.py', content: 'a' }, 'tu_3'),
294	      result(3, 'tu_3', { path: 'src/limits.py', bytes_written: 1, sha256: 'y' }),
295	    ])
296	    expect(recoveredCount(s)).toBe(1)
297	  })
298	
299	  it('matches run_command argv tokens against the fault path', () => {
300	    reset()
301	    const s = reduceAll([call(1, 'run_command', { command: 'cat /workspace/README.md' }, 'tu_1')])
302	    const c = allCalls(s)[0]!
303	    expect(callTouches(c, 'README.md')).toBe(true)
304	    expect(callTouches(c, 'CHANGELOG.md')).toBe(false)
305	  })
306	
307	  it('leaves a fault unrecovered when nothing later touches the path', () => {
308	    reset()
309	    const s = reduceAll([
310	      call(1, 'read_file', { path: 'config/settings.json' }, 'tu_1'),
311	      result(1, 'tu_1', { error: 'No such file', code: 'ENOENT' }, true, {
312	        step: 1,
313	        kind: 'missing_file',
314	        path: 'config/settings.json',
315	        mode: 'sticky',
316	      }),
317	      call(2, 'run_command', { command: 'python -m pytest -q' }, 'tu_2'),
318	      result(2, 'tu_2', { stdout: 'fail', stderr: '', exit_code: 1, duration_ms: 9 }),
319	    ])
320	    expect(recoveredCount(s)).toBe(0)
321	  })
322	})
323	
324	describe('fromRunRecord', () => {
325	  it('lets record-level fields win over the folded events', () => {
326	    reset()
327	    const rec: RunRecord = {
328	      run_id: 'r_1',
329	      status: 'truncated',
330	      scenario_id: 'locked-file',
331	      model: 'claude-sonnet-5',
332	      seed: 3,
333	      max_steps: 5,
334	      episode_id: 'ep_9',
335	      created_at: '2026-09-12T17:00:00.000Z',
336	      finished_at: '2026-09-12T17:00:30.000Z',
337	      events: [ev('run.started', { scenario_id: 'locked-file', model: 'claude-haiku-4-5' })],
338	      evaluation: null,
339	      usage: { input_tokens: 5, output_tokens: 1 },
340	      error: null,
341	    }
342	    const s = fromRunRecord(rec)
343	    expect(s.status).toBe('truncated')
344	    expect(s.model).toBe('claude-sonnet-5')
345	    expect(s.maxSteps).toBe(5)
346	    expect(elapsedMs(s)).toBe(30_000)
347	  })
348	})
